### **2: Minimal Coin Sending Demo** 

Instead of using key-value maps for list of block code, here we use arrays and number indexes, making code less self explanatory but much smaller. The 'outputs' property of the 'buildTx' API function allows you to define multi-asset transaction outputs. In Cardano assets are defined by a policyId and an assetName, for ADA we use 'ada' in both cases. Receiver, asset and quantity are exposed as flat arguments so an auto-generated dapp can drive them

- [try dapp](https://gamechangerfinance.github.io/gamechanger.wallet/examples/Minimal%20Coin%20Sending%20Demo.html)        
- [documentation](Minimal%20Coin%20Sending%20Demo.md)     
- [standalone](https://wallet.gamechanger.finance/api/2/run/1-H4sIAAAAAAAAA1VTTW_aQBT8K0--OJUAEVpA5BYlh3BoFbXcqhyevc_2lvWuvR9gB_Hf-9aGhOADaD0zb2beckp831DykLjcysYnk8RLr-LBT6lljQqejNTwh7SQuoRnqg1jBI1waTQjt9p5QgGmgOAiak_99IAqENTYOCiMBSWdj4BMmXwPuRE0gYoswZGYRIDWYu8AtQAd6owsSC2oIzdhjX0UjRxQ5Bw4UgVQ1yjU6I3tIQse6pBX4NiwIjuDXUWQmuCb4F0KjTUNWd9HAz6-yYJUYtel8Pi6hSLoPCYB5pqjg94E8AYEFVJzgqC8nKJz5MFb1A5H8EV8BlsNT2gFagMDikNwrJEtIOsBoTFK5v1WDPFQj7hfWHMHsZvH58drDSkKTDk6ZMZXkKMjnvCbcpIHspOROKi0ATVvqh-GcRfG8TDkrhUywJahJs1WnBnmBW-mJWmy6BkmsGlYW4OwLBsbqXmnTHLJwylhKAphuWhebfx1324KxLrd7FfL5bo80Pcl1l60TbXZVJt24bvqfb7OK-zKVTgeS7H6tzrMi3z-Qy-pKhebRd8dHH8dyNm2rbu20M26D0e97tv8PbRHy-PHjqZSDFMxGophp5prmlbUfRxfk_PB_Xz4JOdJYgPfxL-n622-LDhe5y6GumxrgHymO5Xk79IYfPaZOv12vg4f8dftfSV8-L3Bx5W-DFZvgF9jjOibDDfI62nEnN_4OU8-AjlZ6l3nhv-eR6lIvJKtpXN8F9lmgcpRDBstXzRzzCuazWe-exnnvt3qhayWflQcWF9I9xcL_wEFb_wJHgQAAA)



### **3: NFT Minting Demo** 

It creates a native script and use it as policy to mint an NFT. Raise 'mint-quantity' and you will be minting Semi-fungible tokens (SFT). Image has been previously uploaded and pinned on IPFS. NFTs contains references to the actual images. Asset name, quantities, policy lifetime, receiver and every CIP-25 metadata field are exposed as flat arguments so an auto-generated dapp can drive the whole mint, and they are checked with assert() before the wallet builds anything

- [try dapp](https://gamechangerfinance.github.io/gamechanger.wallet/examples/NFT%20Minting%20Demo.html)        
- [documentation](NFT%20Minting%20Demo.md)     
- ### **4: Deploy Plutus Script** 

Demo that sends a transaction that includes an inlined Plutus Script and also a datum on outputs. Future transactions can reference this outputs without having to include on-chain the entire data again. A GameChanger Wallet Dapp Demo. https://gamechanger.finance/

### **8: Forty Two (typed)** 

This contract will lock up some tokens that can only be unlocked providing 42 as typed redeemer and datum.. A GameChanger Wallet Dapp Demo. https://gamechanger.finance/

- [try dapp](https://gamechangerfinance.github.io/gamechanger.wallet/examples/Forty%20Two%20(typed).html)

### **10: UTXO Generator**

### **11: Helios Timelock** 

Builds an Helios Smart Contract on the fly. First transaction will lock coins setting main staking key as owner, second one will be able to unlock it only after some time and using main spending key. Burner/Gift wallets not recommended. Timelock not exactly tested.

- [try dapp](https://gamechangerfinance.github.io/gamechanger.wallet/examples/Helios%20Timelock.html)        
- [documentation](Helios%20Timelock.md)

### **12: Helios One-Shot Mint** 

Parameterizes and compiles an Helios Smart Contract on the fly on user wallet, a One-Shot Mint validator pattern. The UTXO required for the one-shot mint is produced on a first transaction, but a previously-generated one can be provided to avoid this first step.

- [try dapp](https://gamechangerfinance.github.io/gamechanger.wallet/examples/Helios%20One-Shot%20Mint.html)

### **13: Plutus Script Parametrization** 

Parameterizes a Plutus Script with same original parameters and verifies the hashes remains equal. Modify parameters to trigger a hash mismatch error. Remove 'strictScriptHashHex' to allow for the new parametrization

- [try dapp](https://gamechangerfinance.github.io/gamechanger.wallet/examples/Plutus%20Script%20Parametrization.html)

### **40: Load Wallet Config Demo** 

Loads a wallet configuration with five workspaces: default, multi-delegation, hierarchical deterministic, anonymous, and anonymous multi-delegation. Child keys are derived and the addresses each workspace needs are built, then the loaded profile is exported. Workspaces are virtual groups of keys and addresses that give users a specialized wallet experience. See 'Save Wallet Config Demo' to persist one

- [try dapp](https://gamechangerfinance.github.io/gamechanger.wallet/examples/Load%20Wallet%20Config%20Demo.html)        
- [documentation](Load%20Wallet%20Config%20Demo.md)

### **41: Save Wallet Config Demo** 

Script that will save wallet artifacts on default wallet settings GCFS DiskNFT. Settings can be derived child keys, built child addresses and even setup address books, all these inside different wallet workspaces. Workspaces are virtual groups of artifacts that allow users to have a specialized wallet experience like multi-delegation, import a Yoroi or Daedalus wallet, work with enhanced anonymity, or whatever config a dapp requires.

- [try dapp](https://gamechangerfinance.github.io/gamechanger.wallet/examples/Save%20Wallet%20Config%20Demo.html)        
- [documentation](Save%20Wallet%20Config%20Demo.md)

### **43: CIP-8 Data Signing and Encrypting Demo** 

Demo that signs data with main and current addresses and their associated reward ones, verifies the signatures and encrypt and decrypt the data using the signatures. Run 'Wallet Setup Demo' or create some Workspaces first and switch current addresses to test it out

### **49: Delegate Current Address to PEACE (MAINNET)**

### **50: Child Keys Multisign** 

Derives three stake keys at arbitrary account and address indexes of the current wallet, and requires all three of them as signers of a single transaction. Shows how child keys outside the usual account 0 / address 0 path can co-sign, without setting up a workspace first

- [try dapp](https://gamechangerfinance.github.io/gamechanger.wallet/examples/Child%20Keys%20Multisign.html)

### **52: Multi Stake Withdrawal** 

Script that requests 3 child wallet's reward addresses from an specific workspace, to later withdraw some staking rewards from each of them on a single transaction. You need to have staking rewards on each child wallet and run 'Multi Stake Delegation' demo first or load its wallet configuration on this device

- [try dapp](https://gamechangerfinance.github.io/gamechanger.wallet/examples/Multi%20Stake%20Withdrawal.html)        
- [documentation](Multi%20Stake%20Withdrawal.md)

### **53: Stake Delegation** 

Registers the derived stake key and delegates it to a stake pool in a single transaction, then returns the delegated address so it can be funded. The target stake pool is exposed as a flat argument, so an auto-generated dapp can delegate to any pool without touching the code, and it is checked with assert() before the wallet builds anything

- [try dapp](https://gamechangerfinance.github.io/gamechanger.wallet/examples/Stake%20Delegation.html)        
- [documentation](Stake%20Delegation.md)     
- [standalone](https://wallet.gamechanger.finance/api/2/run/1-H4sIAAAAAAAAA61WS2_bOBD-K4QusgHZcPyI09yMbLAtFtgNktzSHChqZHMtUypJJTaM_PedGUmOlNp1u-jJFjnzzTdv7gO_KyC4DpyyuvBBFHjtMzp48HIN4g_IYCm9zg1eJVBJ0dd1cA9L7TxYJ_wKRAJWv0AiHKutYSekSfCU1cEJ7YXPhazvizzPhDb0rc0yA-GtNE4qgo4IzwgLvrSmAa9gEiGTxIJzwuWEqKQRMYi0NAkkQ_GIol7aJfiOGSdgW-SOtB1aTDPpBUqVGzA-IiREkaXPB0swYNlMIouC0RvLTN7sKsRX7Vd5SQ6VaoX8maPKE4jYaSSGNtUK1BqhSBgNO7C-10e2aW6BFV5lliHTuNRZ4gjcExaGmdhav3CUBI5OnQTAOwvfSm0xP_vA5J5-KphHzOIXzMpTEGPUwKLoUqc-eH57IyUKJQlvkCTCZtL5AC8wCo6OOVoD8m2AmRuspFuhVJyMp_FkMh_PP12k09GFUqPxp-lspqZTkPN5OlFX89lIwSS-Gk3lZDaV6ShNLy7RdgvQawyDRbi728XNLVm1JXNpUvBnKW3CNOpS3Ehl8-Agd4TcYFM6P4hhIAfOW4radbBvgozBp2j0wuoqjLAeeiG5OjwCFfb7IgqPXAiyQdVFNctAIhIHqLAv-uKt62mXHaaHGMKm8LsOPbzoaXdL5w8M3DtH8IcMEY5Ysp1fpIj0ZpcDtZLWdRhiEffg2zlakccEKSzLs4Kzy2gUhehIZH4r7KyGPZdB2OJkyXZifCXiHQ2j3uxSrGAr2PX-_0tsDGo1GXczi979NPtpRT4K6SI85wINDCtfmTXPIJqwJBFxAUhR0aknHo9h62k08fj5GtD5xdfgmKs0IRIoAGeoURranXhYCnUrqtJa7NhFNYRbgoh5072j6VL9_WLS_FR3B3vUXLzLVVFTEofnsM1p2LWMOSfePNpoCVneUDeYhJYhhZ861aoanKq-XGuTNNvtvqWMIkUZ_wW7zxjUz7CtuR0l03KMk4txrhXDfhPPZm3-MqnOxv1NlCLO_xGk0_VZ-eG3R8uh2hstjMpIXSW80Q47612dzx-3rTfG_t9cm14oeEYvrJW7Xvjx3YFb9sQIrzYL9T8Z91uu0Pf4IsOn6uD72H0sG_Y3OiXdzSbLPkdBzg8hjg-9Hu5s_qIdP41wKed27Qqp4G9Ue4GHKnDXOByAGgPF_2FtmT3oJa5rRjGnZG8ykKbx7r5Ttq4W5GZwiHUk7nT8iImksvRSZ5Dcgd1o5yr9VGYO6lQ_dfzGo2EnmXiC0aEAPJO5Mt5of8wgX1QmGfY71DbTsOnlVGM8st3pl4Bs5s7pPvg4J7gs4RVfGIvzyu0m6ihVOFx6d--N9FNdRBHoCP8gtIfGe3v7D8xFkTiYCwAA)



### **54: Stake Withdrawal** 

Withdraws staking rewards from the reward account of the current address. Cardano requires a withdrawal to move the entire reward balance, so the quantity must match the rewards available at build time. It is exposed as a flat argument, so an auto-generated dapp can drive it without touching the code, and it is checked with assert() before the wallet builds anything

- [try dapp](https://gamechangerfinance.github.io/gamechanger.wallet/examples/Stake%20Withdrawal.html)        
- [documentation](Stake%20Withdrawal.md)     
- [standalone](https://wallet.gamechanger.finance/api/2/run/1-H4sIAAAAAAAAA6VVTW_jNhD9K4QusgF_Fb35li6KboBimzYpeljsgZbGEhtK1JKjJK7h_943lOTIidLsor7Emc837w3Hx4QPDSXbJGTeNJwsEjZsxXDL-p7UX4bL3OtHbeHKqYsyrkbA4AoqINTUhfL0qH0e1N67SnFJvUHpLHNtzcrtozVrvSf8q_PcUwgr9QFBunYI_9oamJRWj-e-ip2q3APFVKQhYKi701bXGS1UcNH7tdXw80FVbWBVac7KEQyUfdDG6p0lpVntWmNzxaailbpmZYKip8YFAlwBsLeI0b5oK_SMHXStdMtuWVBNXjMCc900KoM99wYADUfYrmVgbrNSKInzuhwYdZ1LBPpkJWX3SJdgNAvkeTZXO9o7302JqS31CIGlPrDUggCC0PNVEHlgsTRoAF9PXrI9JrVj-dOVuYO-19Drc7JrPZAjtDB7Tr6cTpLEMP75x683mpm8yFoyN9v12rpM29IF3v642WzWOVVurRuzlpmTIVG6VJgOaVYHTlARlIXY_CzgcpAFUT9s4kcCfRvTB4p_aUUisfQLWenMu-QcN1FvKTIvd7TUy8BeGNomx4FQEC2Tz9LOlS4K4lkq6FYTpdL5XC3SCUe3SjtsjOoLqYU6l0rnaq5OAPkmOkghCKlq4vzP8OCYmfCz2G9j4dl7AP8TIcoJytjnOyECHvbeMFb4AmFWNT-Z4lNbzdKC3-VvkW7S9zgsPOHdeOw43sw_5F33KqIb2ytvXba_dPblE3_r6U6NKnudU0N1TnVmaLxT5yPXL1V_ia66QzQKRM0Plz5Z7e7rdb13b-1pckTm1XNcJ2qm8eRXY0yry85QV3CjBz9N4u1eVaw-zHp6HiPycT4Fz-nRfvc0OurHv52pZ6mKcl55rw-z9OWhx5n-BrUtTrLVGUXogvvykcYp-rsbn_lAcT_DBCUjeldd5pkd6TC6Isd30HU74OIPVdcdd_vGuwcT4k8XgDp_HxrA_6Rl7287nrfsWxKhEf5bzNb21hQ4mrFK_To2ahYQMsG-mO8gp2wjY3spvyFfmRA6VHuQRL3gny9YgWl1ISksH-lJ5voi7dpdZXiqYXR0LWPZV1XHSNNh5fYGY9rD26f3G7R7uc5xOcca_g_hB4nr379nA4SCjzqUEyy84hZhPRunfwGeERtAEAkAAA)



### **55: Lock and Redeem Smart Contract** 

Coin locking transaction ( 5 eUTXOs) and later an unlocking one of previously locked coin, in 2 secuential transactions. Using deployed smart contract.

### **56: Native asset minter and burner** 

Creates a native script and uses it as policy to mint two fungible tokens under it, then burns one of them in a second transaction while the other survives. Asset names and quantities are checked with assert() before the wallet builds anything, remove them to explore different outcomes and balance states.

- [try dapp](https://gamechangerfinance.github.io/gamechanger.wallet/examples/Native%20asset%20minter%20and%20burner.html)        
- [documentation](Native%20asset%20minter%20and%20burner.md)

### **57: Build Certificates** 

Builds stake registration, deregistration and delegation certificates for the three kinds of stake credential: a key derived from the current wallet, a native script and a Plutus script. Nothing is submitted: the certificates are exported so they can be inspected or reused in a transaction. The target pool of each delegation is exposed as a flat argument, and checked with assert() before anything is built

- [try dapp](https://gamechangerfinance.github.io/gamechanger.wallet/examples/Build%20Certificates.html)        
- [documentation](Build%20Certificates.md)     
- [standalone](https://wallet.gamechanger.finance/api/2/run/1-H4sIAAAAAAAAA8VXbW_aSBD-Kyt_IUiGrN9tvl1b6Vrd6VS16afr6bQvY7BibLpep0ER_72ztqHmgiGE5BopgHdmZ-aZeXZ5eLD0egXWzKqEylbasi2d6dwsvKmzXJK3oHSWZoJpqNAoofXLymLrUpFKs1sgCuZZpRUzNptI6D8TVkhcymHePopeVJKWiugF4L8CILdZgSHLtIsqFEgodMbyGWHkFtYmcnYHkqSqXDb7RK0UupDvLM9B2-hWYJY7IG2pTW5GPua1rqtubUr-KvUiK-Ykw6WaLzOtQc7acP3amAIC96tSoZlUpXFYE8EKwoFkRbUCYQwIQEFd4acMoRIEXVRMGKRTcmOQMTUHTVZlmRtkwMSi3w2sweQw-xmmJGnOsGo1r5cIy27qFwsQt2j_nukFOlVY49UYi8DeATqsd2A4jsRMUcG3OlM4xwerKLV5a9tzg9P-gLP724J7DUVlBvnPZmM26FoVXz79-ZFhL5QZ70Lr1ez6Oi8FyxdlpWcepfRawrK8ZqvsWrLVCjNhoZWJj_jVpBnaxACd4KwmC1YtMFACPAo5DxJHhnEiJQ096osoElSmURw5-BpCFMZBFIEbstSh0nM5FYaNClhVq_VA5DR1PeYyRzgRUNejLIxF7PrCixiNY6CMp0kaBq6b-pFHvTByktTzQ9pQ-X4gaChjTwpPCE9S4dHQjambCB473HEcPw4ZTygPuJRcxFJKSPwwCpzIs0wb68I0Yzu932umZNOe7pgtmVCltfMbatpkWVd6wmEShBOxYApDWA_bwRtGXMG3K-TU1ci0fzoUZjS2NWYyXH6adxDa1B6NxmO7eKUEQZdgTOzRkB8x6M0Rg3s8RvmauDHha3Mer4KQLOCeND0Zj4hNdllHYzImG2zt8Z7iaTB95SAWnrvXVYP4PDB-i8UeGcPoSYjMDaPY9wZEcx-YK8142AQrw8PfFtbaMmluQbyO8Gw3J_-rZdadr9Zh5McOy5mMOhbq8NBP7Bhi1ssn6jPsmO9lLDvd67OZdgLYY7Y9Cd2rMm7gEj2TbANRDo9_2HmIYi8Vvk-sAbfLOHW0m2fTaRjJYyadgvOKJDLio9F0jRjqf1e2Su8PWBvleWve0Pam5I2oYctGpOIT2lAzGglrABhFIkRZF_pDgaismefjipQKqqpb8V1v02ZdAa4UIoN-2p0S7r6jWzX5uVmlTs-xb0D3bh_a20_vTS4rdqkTe_jqOwzlUyATKvGJBjGKFsdJRSJEmErP59KNYlRYDvdSnkbcD0IOPPGYF0vXZw7QMPBdic4c9wdNtBgSmjQdrJbY8LdlgdpT7Fe5aoTvgSpzVsx39n_v3J2xLdwHX1IHk1DPc80f1uzgS_PvYNJWNf4U-eanwsE-tgL6N3MJfHrkv2vzFyRzb3dPhqOPeTLGbtKfzaT7sdBlVXOkynvkZFv_Q0M0gVobpj1-TZEy0z3f0bgl4U13m15SRNfAgSJ-0m26T6rp3r5tQe9MiP-hlv9w51Ax3SE9c9rvDux4_rz3o_2yiT8q45fO_PnVPHnq29-pT5v4vvf252FXRnvtby4iwTbBpQSwmy-lA_tPqP6LqNMr_sVocxzIcVH5XNI9B8dJwh0HMihnWqZuNj8A_HaQscsSAAA)



### **58: Wallet derivation script** 

Derives six spending and six staking keys of the current wallet and builds every kind of address they allow: a reward address, hierarchical deterministic, delegation portfolio and anonymous addresses, native script multisig treasuries and Plutus smart contract addresses. Nothing is submitted on-chain and every address is exported back to dapp for inspection

### **75: Shared Treasury - 3 of 4 signers** 

Creates a workspace named 'DAO' with a 'Shared Treasury' address where at least 3 of 4 signer wallets are required for spending and staking operations. A second address on the workspace is caller main address as 'Signer'. To manage the treasury set 'Shared Treasury' address as current. To sign as a member, set 'Signer' address instead. Please customize this code with real public key hashes, you can use 'Share your public key information' example on each member to obtain them. Please use 'saveConfig' function to save this setup on GCFS to avoid losing funds and data

- [try dapp](https://gamechangerfinance.github.io/gamechanger.wallet/examples/Shared%20Treasury%20-%203%20of%204%20signers.html)        
- [documentation](Shared%20Treasury%20-%203%20of%204%20signers.md)

### **77: Shared Treasury - 3 of 4 signers - funded - Gift Wallets** 

Creates a workspace named 'DAO' with a 'Shared Treasury' address where at least 3 of 4 signer Gift wallets are required for spending and staking operations. A second address on the workspace is caller main address as 'Signer'. To manage the treasury set 'Shared Treasury' address as current. To sign as a member, set 'Signer' address instead. Customize Gift Wallet generation with proper password settings and distribute them to the signers. This example is not secure as wallet creator has total control over the generated wallets. Please use 'saveConfig' function to save this setup on GCFS to avoid losing funds and data

- [try dapp](https://gamechangerfinance.github.io/gamechanger.wallet/examples/Shared%20Treasury%20-%203%20of%204%20signers%20-%20funded%20-%20Gift%20Wallets.html)

### **79: Simple Kobayashi Maru Multi Signing Demo** 

Builds and signs 2 transactions with unobtainable foreign keys. Triggers the default multisig strategy to obtain remote signatures through Roundtable and Unimatrix

### **80: Smart Handles - Swap ADA to MIN on Minswap**

### **98: Batch Airdrop with Receipt** 

Airdrops an existing native asset to five recipients in a single transaction and stamps a CIP-20 receipt on-chain: a SHA-256 digest of the canonical recipient manifest. Anyone can later recompute the digest from the published list and prove the drop was not altered, without trusting the sender. Minimum ADA per output is auto-computed by buildTx, and the change optimizer is left enabled to keep the UTXO set healthy.

- [try dapp](https://gamechangerfinance.github.io/gamechanger.wallet/examples/Batch%20Airdrop%20with%20Receipt.html)        
- [documentation](Batch%20Airdrop%20with%20Receipt.md)

### **99: CIP-68 NFT Mint** 

Mints a CIP-68 token pair under one native script policy: a (100) reference NFT that carries the metadata as an inline datum, and a (222) user NFT that the holder actually owns. Unlike CIP-25, the metadata lives in an eUTXO datum instead of transaction metadata, so it can be updated later by whoever controls the reference token under the script-controlled address that holds it. The datum follows the CIP-68 shape #6.121([metadata, version, extra]) with 'extra' set to Unit/Void. Every value a dapp would want to change is exposed as a flat argument, and after submitting, the wallet redirects to a block explorer that renders CIP-68 tokens so the result can be checked. ONE DELIBERATE SIMPLIFICATION, for example purposes: uniqueness of the reference NFT rests on issuer behaviour plus the policy time-lock, not on a one-shot policy that consumes a specific UTXO, so the policy could technically mint another (100) token before it expires. A production issuer should use a one-shot minting policy. 

- [try dapp](https://gamechangerfinance.github.io/gamechanger.wallet/examples/CIP-68%20NFT%20Mint.html)

### **100: Token-Gated Sign-In** 

Backend-less 'sign in with Cardano' for native asset holders only. The wallet blocks on an await/balance condition until the gating token is actually held, then signs a dapp-supplied nonce with CIP-8 and verifies its own signature before returning. The dapp learns the bare-minimum to verify: the realm, the address, the proof and nothing else.

- [try dapp](https://gamechangerfinance.github.io/gamechanger.wallet/examples/Token-Gated%20Sign-In.html)        
- [documentation](Token-Gated%20Sign-In.md)
